- Given a Toolbar (react-aria-components) given a custom render prop returning a <div> with an extra data-custom attribute, when it renders, then the resulting toolbar element (role="toolbar") carries the custom data-custom="true" attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toolbar.test.tsx: "should support custom render function")
- Given a Toolbar (react-aria-components) whose children are provided as a render-prop function receiving {orientation}, when it renders with no explicit orientation set, then the render prop reports "horizontal" as the default orientation value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toolbar.test.tsx: "support render props")
- Given a Toolbar (Spectrum v3) with two ActionGroups joined by a Divider (one with onAction, one selection-mode with onSelectionChange), when Tab focuses the first item and Enter is pressed, then onAction receives the correct item key ("alignleft") as its argument, confirming action callback wiring is preserved when ActionGroups are composed inside a Toolbar. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/actiongroup/Toolbar.test.tsx: "support actions and selection")

## group

### Roles and attributes

- Given a react-aria-components Group rendered with no className, when queried by role, then it renders with role="group" and the default class "react-aria-Group". (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should render a group with default class")
- Given a react-aria-components Group rendered with className="test", when queried by role, then the group element's class attribute is exactly "test", overriding the default class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should render a group with custom class")
- Given a react-aria-components Group rendered with an arbitrary data-foo="bar" prop, when queried by role, then the group element carries that DOM attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support DOM props")
- Given a react-aria-components Group rendered with a custom render prop supplying a <div> with data-custom="true", when queried by role, then the resulting group element carries data-custom="true", confirming the custom render function is used. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support custom render function")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a react-aria-components Group whose className is a function of isHovered with onHoverStart/onHoverChange/onHoverEnd handlers, when not hovered, then the group has no data-hovered attribute and no hover class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support hover")
- Given that same Group, when the pointer hovers it, then data-hovered="true" and the hover class are applied and onHoverStart/onHoverChange each fire once; when the pointer unhovers it, then both are removed and onHoverEnd fires once while onHoverChange's total reaches two. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support hover")
- Given a react-aria-components Group whose children is a render-prop function returning "Hovered" or "Group" based on isHovered, when the pointer hovers the group, then its text content changes to "Hovered", and when unhovered, then it reverts to "Group". (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support render props")

### Focus management

- Given a react-aria-components Group containing a text input, with className a function of isFocusVisible, when the user tabs to focus the inner input, then the group gains data-focus-visible="true" and the focus class even though focus lands on the child input rather than the group itself. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support focus ring")
- Given that same Group with the input focused and focus-visible, when Tab moves focus away, then data-focus-visible and the focus class are removed from the group. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support focus ring")
- Given a react-aria-components Group containing a text input with className a function of isFocusVisible, when the user clicks into the input (pointer modality) and then types a character, then the input receives the typed value but the group does NOT gain data-focus-visible or the focus class — the focus ring is suppressed for pointer-modality focus. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should not show focus ring when typing in pointer modality")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a react-aria-components Group rendered with isDisabled and a className function of isDisabled, when queried by role, then the group carries data-disabled="true" and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support disabled state")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a GroupContext.Provider supplying slots: {test: {'aria-label': 'test'}} and a Group with slot="test" inside it, when queried by role, then the group element receives both slot="test" and aria-label="test" merged in from the slotted context value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Group.test.tsx: "should support slot")

## checkbox

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered with isInvalid, when queried by role, then the checkbox carries aria-invalid="true". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be invalid")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered with isInvalid and an explicit aria-errormessage, when queried by role, then aria-invalid is "true" and aria-errormessage is passed through to the input. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name passes through aria-errormessage")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered with an aria-label and no visible text, when queried by role, then the checkbox carries that aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can have a non-visible label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered next to an external labelling span with aria-labelledby, when queried by role, then the checkbox exposes that aria-labelledby value. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports aria-labelledby")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered next to an external describing span with aria-describedby, when queried by role, then the checkbox exposes that aria-describedby value. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports aria-describedby")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered with a data-testid prop, when queried by test id, then the rendered label element carries the extra DOM prop. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports additional props")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered isReadOnly with a className function keyed on isReadOnly, when inspected, then the input has aria-readonly="true" and the label and its wrapper carry data-readonly and the readonly class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support read only state")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered isInvalid with a className function keyed on isInvalid, when inspected, then the input has aria-invalid="true" and the label and its wrapper carry data-invalid="true" and the invalid class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support invalid state")
- Given the RAC CheckboxField layer rendered with a description prop, when inspected, then the checkbox input has aria-describedby pointing at an element whose text content is the description. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support description")
- Given the RAC CheckboxField layer rendered without a description, when the description prop is added and then removed via rerender, then aria-describedby is added on the input and then removed again. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should update aria-describedby when changing description prop")

### Keyboard

- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a className function keyed on isPressed and onPress/onClick handlers, when tabbing to the checkbox and pressing then releasing Space, then data-pressed and the pressed class appear while Space is held and clear on release, and onPress/onClick each fire once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support press state with keyboard")
- Given the RAC Checkbox layer rendered inside a native form with a submit button, when the checkbox is clicked to focus it and Enter is pressed, then the form submits once via implicit submission. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support implicit form submission from a focused checkbox on Enter")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with onHoverStart/onHoverChange/onHoverEnd handlers and a className function keyed on isHovered, when hovering then unhovering the label, then data-hovered and the hover class toggle on and off and each hover callback fires the expected number of times. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support hover")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a className function keyed on isPressed and onPress/onClick/onClickCapture handlers, when pressing then releasing the mouse button on the label, then data-pressed and the pressed class toggle across the press and each handler fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support press state")

### Focus management

- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered with excludeFromTabOrder, when queried by role, then the checkbox input has tabIndex="-1". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports excludeFromTabOrder")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a className function keyed on isFocusVisible, when tabbing onto then away from the checkbox, then the label gains data-focus-visible and the focus class while keyboard-focused and loses both once focus moves on. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support focus ring")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with onFocus/onFocusChange/onBlur handlers next to another focusable button, when tabbing onto the checkbox then tabbing to the button, then onFocus and onFocusChange(true) fire once on entry and onBlur and onFocusChange(false) fire once on exit. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support focus events")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with onFocus/onBlur handlers, when clicking the label twice in a row, then onFocus fires only on the first click and onBlur never fires between the two clicks since focus never leaves the checkbox. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should not trigger onBlur/onFocus on sequential presses")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered unchecked by default, when clicked twice, then it becomes checked with onChange called with true, then unchecked with onChange called with false. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name default unchecked can be checked")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered with defaultSelected and a custom value, when inspected then clicked, then the native input's value matches the given value and starts checked, and clicking unchecks it while onChange fires with false. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be default checked")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered controlled with isSelected=true, when clicked, then the checkbox remains checked because the controlled prop does not change, while onChange still fires with false. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be controlled checked")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered controlled with isSelected=false, when clicked, then the checkbox remains unchecked because the controlled prop does not change, while onChange still fires with true. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be controlled unchecked")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered with isDisabled, when clicked, then it stays unchecked and onChange is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be disabled")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox (plain and isEmphasized variants) rendered with isIndeterminate, when inspected then clicked twice, then the DOM indeterminate property stays true across both clicks while checked toggles true then false and onChange fires with true then false. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name can be indeterminate")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered controlled with isSelected=true and isReadOnly, when clicked, then it stays checked and onChange is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports readOnly")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered uncontrolled with isReadOnly, when clicked, then it stays unchecked and onChange is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "$Name supports uncontrolled readOnly")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with isDisabled and a className function keyed on isDisabled, when inspected, then the input is disabled and the label and its wrapper carry data-disabled="true" and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support disabled state")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered uncontrolled with onChange and a className function keyed on isSelected, when clicked twice, then checked state, data-selected, and the selected class toggle on then off, and onChange is called with true then false. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support selected state")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with isIndeterminate and a className function keyed on isIndeterminate, when inspected, then the input's indeterminate DOM property is true and the label carries data-indeterminate and the indeterminate class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support indeterminate state")

### Forms (name, value, validation, form reset)

- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox inside a native form with a reset button, when the checkbox is clicked to check it and the form is then reset, then the checkbox reverts to unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports form reset")
- Given React 19's useActionState driving a form action prop (only run when React version is 19 or newer) with a Checkbox bound to defaultSelected from the action state, when the submit button runs the form action, then the checkbox becomes checked to reflect the updated defaultSelected value. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "resets to defaultSelected when submitting form action")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox with isRequired and validationBehavior="native" inside a Form, when rendered unchecked, then the input has the required attribute (not aria-required) and validity.valid is false; after the form's checkValidity is invoked and the checkbox is clicked, then validity.valid becomes true. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports isRequired")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox with validationBehavior="native" and a custom validate function inside a Form, when rendered unchecked, then validity.valid is false; after checkValidity is invoked and the checkbox is clicked, then validity.valid becomes true. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox with validationBehavior="native", a name, and a Form whose submit handler sets server validationErrors, when the submit button is clicked, then the checkbox is invalid (validity.valid false); when the checkbox is then clicked to check it, then validity.valid becomes true. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox with the default validationBehavior="aria" and a custom validate function, when rendered, then validity.valid is always true because aria validation does not drive native constraint validity. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox with the default validationBehavior="aria", a name, and a Form with server validationErrors, when rendered, then the checkbox has aria-invalid="true"; when the checkbox is clicked, then aria-invalid is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered isRequired inside a Form with a className function keyed on isRequired, when inspected, then the input has the required attribute and the label and wrapper carry data-required="true" and the required class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support required state")
- Given the RAC CheckboxField layer rendered isRequired inside a Form, when the form's requestSubmit is called without checking the box, then the checkbox gains aria-describedby referencing a "Constraints not satisfied" error message. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support required state")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered isRequired inside a Form after a failed submit, when the label is clicked to check the box, then aria-invalid, data-invalid, and aria-describedby are all cleared. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support required state")
- Given the RAC Checkbox layer rendered with a form="test" prop, when inspected, then the checkbox input carries a form attribute of "test". (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support form prop")

### SSR and hydration

- Given a Spectrum v3 Checkbox rendered through the SSR test harness, when server-rendering `<Checkbox>Test</Checkbox>`, then it renders without errors. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/Checkbox.ssr.test.js: "should render without errors")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with no custom class, when inspected, then the wrapper element carries the default class "react-aria-Checkbox" or "react-aria-CheckboxField" respectively. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should render a checkbox with default class")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a custom className string, when inspected, then the wrapper element's class is replaced by that custom class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should render a checkbox with custom class")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with an arbitrary DOM prop such as data-foo, when inspected, then the wrapper element receives that attribute but the inner checkbox input does not. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support DOM props")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a custom render function supplying the root element, when inspected, then the custom root element receives the component's computed props including a data-custom attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support custom render function")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered inside a context provider that maps a named slot to extra props (an aria-label), when the component is given slot="test", then the wrapper carries slot="test" and the checkbox input receives the slot's aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support slot")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a render-prop function as children keyed on isSelected, when the checkbox is clicked, then the rendered text updates from "Not Selected" to "Selected". (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support render props")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a ref, when inspected, then the ref points at the root label (or CheckboxField wrapper) element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support refs")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with an inputRef, when inspected, then the ref points at the native checkbox input element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support input ref")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with a callback inputRef returning a cleanup function, when the component mounts then unmounts, then the callback fires with the input element on mount and its returned cleanup function runs once on unmount. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support callback ref")
- Given the RAC Checkbox and CheckboxField layers rendered with both a context-level inputRef and a prop-level inputRef, when inspected, then both refs are populated with the same native checkbox input element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Checkbox.test.js: "should support and merge input ref on context")

## checkbox-group

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with three Checkbox children, when rendered, then the group has role="group" containing three checkboxes whose native inputs have no name attribute, each has the value matching its Checkbox's value prop, and all are unchecked by default. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "handles defaults")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with a label prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-labelledby pointing at an element whose text content is the label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports labeling")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with an aria-label prop, when rendered, then the group carries that aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isDisabled, when rendered, then the group has aria-disabled="true" and every checkbox input carries the native disabled attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "sets aria-disabled when isDisabled is true")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with no isDisabled prop set, when rendered, then neither the group nor any checkbox carries a disabled or aria-disabled attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "doesn't set aria-disabled by default")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isDisabled explicitly set to false, when rendered, then neither the group nor any checkbox carries a disabled or aria-disabled attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "doesn't set aria-disabled when isDisabled is false")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isReadOnly, when rendered, then every checkbox input carries aria-readonly="true". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "sets aria-readonly=\"true\" on each checkbox")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isRequired on the group only, when rendered, then the group's label gains a "(required)" necessity indicator image while none of the individual checkbox inputs receive a required attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "adds required to group label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isRequired on both the group and each individual Checkbox, when rendered, then the group's label shows the "(required)" indicator and every checkbox input carries aria-required. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports isRequired on individual checkboxes")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isInvalid, when rendered, then every checkbox input carries aria-invalid. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "adds aria-invalid to all checkboxes when the group is invalid")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup where isInvalid is set on two of three individual Checkbox children, when rendered, then only those two checkbox inputs carry aria-invalid="true" while the third does not. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports invalid state on individual checkboxes")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with a description prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-describedby pointing at an element whose text content is the description. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support help text description")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup and its Checkbox/CheckboxField children rendered invalid with a group-level description Text slot, an errorMessage Text slot, and a per-checkbox description, when inspected, then the group's aria-describedby resolves to "Description Error" and the first checkbox's aria-describedby resolves to its own description plus the group's error and description text, in an order that differs between the CheckboxField and plain Checkbox variants. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports help text")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with an explicit aria-describedby on each Checkbox child, when inspected, then every checkbox input carries that same aria-describedby value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support aria-describedby on a checkbox")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup and its Checkbox/CheckboxField children rendered isReadOnly on the group with a matching className function on the checkboxes, when inspected, then the group and each checkbox root carry data-readonly and the readonly class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support read only state")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup and its Checkbox/CheckboxField children rendered isInvalid on the group with a matching className function on the checkboxes, when inspected, then the group and each checkbox root carry data-invalid="true" and the invalid class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support validation state")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({label: 'Favorite Pet'})` with three `useCheckboxGroupItem` checkboxes, when rendered, then the container has `role="group"`, three `role="checkbox"` inputs exist, none has a `name` attribute, and each input's `value` is its item value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "handles defaults")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({label: 'Favorite Pet'})`, when rendered, then the group's `aria-labelledby` resolves to an element containing the label text. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "supports labeling")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({'aria-label': 'My Favorite Pet'})`, when rendered, then the group carries that `aria-label`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "supports aria-label")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({isDisabled: true})`, when rendered, then the group has `aria-disabled="true"` and every input has the native `disabled` attribute; clicking a label fires neither the group's nor the item's `onChange` and leaves the box unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "sets aria-disabled and makes checkboxes disabled when isDisabled is true")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup` with no `isDisabled`, when rendered, then the group has no `aria-disabled` and no input is `disabled`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "doesn't set aria-disabled or make checkboxes disabled by default")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({isDisabled: false})`, when rendered, then the group has no `aria-disabled` and no input is `disabled`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "doesn't set aria-disabled or make checkboxes disabled when isDisabled is false")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({isReadOnly: true})`, when rendered, then every input has `aria-readonly="true"`, and clicking a label fires neither `onChange` and leaves the value unchanged. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "sets aria-readonly=\"true\" on each checkbox")

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with five checkboxes (two individually disabled) rendered across ltr+vertical, rtl+vertical, ltr+horizontal, and rtl+horizontal locale/orientation combinations (template variants), when toggling checkboxes by index, by keyboard interaction, by label text, and by mouse via the CheckboxGroup test-util tester, then each targeted checkbox toggles correctly, disabled checkboxes are skipped, and the tester's selected-checkbox count updates as expected regardless of orientation or RTL locale. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "$Name should select the correct checkbox regardless of orientation and disabled checkboxes")
- Given a Spectrum 2 CheckboxGroup with five checkboxes (two individually disabled) rendered across ltr+vertical, rtl+vertical, ltr+horizontal, and rtl+horizontal locale/orientation combinations (template variants), when toggling checkboxes by index, by keyboard interaction, by label text, and by mouse via the CheckboxGroup test-util tester, then each targeted checkbox toggles correctly, disabled checkboxes are skipped, and the tester's selected-checkbox count updates as expected regardless of orientation or RTL locale. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/CheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "$Name should select the correct checkbox regardless of orientation and disabled checkboxes")
- Given a Spectrum 2 CheckboxGroup rendered in a real browser (vitest browser mode) with three described checkboxes, when the CheckboxGroup test-util tester is configured for mouse interaction or for keyboard interaction (two template variants) and toggles the third checkbox, then the tester reports that checkbox as selected in both interaction modes. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/CheckboxGroup.browser.test.tsx: "toggles a checkbox via $interactionType")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a default `useCheckboxGroup` with three items, when the Dragons label is clicked, then the group's `onChange` fires once with `['dragons']` and only that input becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "handles defaults")
- Given a single item with `isReadOnly` inside an otherwise editable group, when that item's label is clicked, then neither the group's nor the item's `onChange` fires and the box stays unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "should not update state for readonly checkbox")

### Focus management

- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with onFocus/onFocusChange/onBlur on the group, multiple checkboxes, and an outer button, when tabbing from outside onto the group's first checkbox then to its second checkbox, then onFocus and onFocusChange(true) fire only once on entering the group and do not fire again as focus moves between checkboxes inside it; when focus then moves to the outer button, then onBlur and onFocusChange(false) fire once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support focus events")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook (react-stately layer) called with no props, when read, then it returns an empty value array, isDisabled and isReadOnly both false, and setValue/addValue/removeValue/toggleValue/isSelected as functions. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should return basic interface when no props are provided")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook called with isDisabled: true, when read, then state.isDisabled is true. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should return the same `isDisabled` that has been provided")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook called with isReadOnly: true, when read, then state.isReadOnly is true. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should return the same `isReadOnly` that has been provided")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook called with a controlled value of ['foo', 'bar'], when read, then state.value equals that array. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to provide the initial value")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook called with defaultValue ['foo', 'bar'], when read, then state.value equals that array. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to provide a default value")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook called with a controlled value of ['foo', 'bar'], when isSelected is called with 'foo' and with 'baz', then it returns true for the present value and false for the absent one. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should support isSelected to determine if a value is selected")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook rendered controlled with a value prop, when the parent rerenders passing a new value array, then state.value reflects the updated array. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to control the value")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook rendered uncontrolled with defaultValue ['foo'], when state.setValue is invoked imperatively, then state.value updates to the new array. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to have the value uncontrolled")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook rendered uncontrolled with defaultValue ['foo'] and an onChange callback, when state.setValue is invoked, then onChange fires with the new value array. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should call the provided `onChange` callback whenever value changes")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with defaultValue ['foo'], when state.addValue('baz') is called, then the value becomes ['foo', 'baz']. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to add a value using `addValue`")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with defaultValue ['foo'], when state.addValue('baz') is called four times in the same update, then 'baz' appears only once in the resulting value (['foo', 'baz']). (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should not add the same value multiple times when using `addValue`")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with defaultValue ['foo', 'qwe'], when state.removeValue('foo') is called, then the value becomes ['qwe']. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to remove a value using `removeValue`")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with defaultValue ['foo', 'qwe'], when state.toggleValue('foo') is called, then 'foo' is removed leaving ['qwe']; when toggled again, then 'foo' is re-added at the end giving ['qwe', 'foo']. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should be possible to add & remove value based on it being or not in the stored value using `toggleValue`")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with defaultValue ['foo', 'qwe'], when state.toggleValue('qwe') is called twice synchronously within the same update batch, then the resulting value is ['foo'] rather than canceling back to the original ['foo', 'qwe'], showing both calls act on the same pre-batch state instead of toggling twice in sequence. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should go back to the initial value after `toggleState` being called twice with the same value synchronously")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with isReadOnly: true and defaultValue ['test'], when addValue, removeValue, toggleValue, and setValue are each called, then the value never changes from ['test']. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should not update state for readonly group")
- Given the bare useCheckboxGroupState hook with isDisabled: true and defaultValue ['test'], when addValue, removeValue, toggleValue, and setValue are each called, then the value never changes from ['test']. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroupState.test.tsx: "should not update state for disabled group")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with a controlled value of ['cats'], when rendered, then the Cats checkbox is checked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "can have a default value")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with three Checkbox children and an onChange handler, when the Dragons checkbox is clicked, then onChange fires once with ['dragons'] and only the Dragons checkbox becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "handles defaults")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with an individually isReadOnly Checkbox and separate onChange spies on the group and that checkbox, when the read-only checkbox is clicked, then neither onChange spy fires and the checkbox stays unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should not update state for readonly checkbox")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with isDisabled on a single Checkbox child and a matching className function, when inspected, then that checkbox's input is disabled and its label/root carry data-disabled="true" and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support disabled state on checkbox")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with isDisabled on the group and a matching className function on both group and checkboxes, when inspected, then the group carries aria-disabled and the disabled class, and every checkbox is disabled with data-disabled and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support disabled state on group")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered uncontrolled with an onChange handler and a className function keyed on isSelected, when the first checkbox is clicked twice, then onChange is called with ['a'] then [], and the checkbox's checked state, data-selected attribute, and selected class toggle to match on each click. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support selected state")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({value: ['cats']})`, when rendered, then the Cats input is checked. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "can have a default value")

### Forms (name, value, validation, form reset)

- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with a name prop, when rendered, then every checkbox's native input carries that name attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "name can be controlled")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isInvalid and an errorMessage prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-describedby pointing at an element whose text content is the error message. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support error message")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup controlled via React state with name="pets" inside a native form with a reset button, when the Cats checkbox is clicked to select it and the form is then reset, then the checkboxes revert to their original checked state (only Dogs checked). (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports form reset")
- Given React 19's useActionState driving a form action prop (only run when React version is 19 or newer) with a CheckboxGroup bound to defaultValue from the action state, when the submit button runs the form action, then Dogs and Cats become checked reflecting the new defaultValue while Dragons stays unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "resets to defaultValue when submitting form action")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with group-level isRequired and validationBehavior="native" inside a Form, when rendered unchecked, then every checkbox input has the required attribute (not aria-required) and reports invalid validity. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level isRequired")
- Given the same group-level required/native-validation CheckboxGroup, when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby referencing "Constraints not satisfied"; when the first checkbox is then clicked to select it, then every checkbox loses required/aria-required and reports valid validity, and the group's aria-describedby is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level isRequired")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with validationBehavior="native" and isRequired set on two of three individual Checkbox children, when rendered, then only those two checkbox inputs are required and invalid while the unrequired third reports valid; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with "Constraints not satisfied" and focus moves to the first invalid checkbox. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level isRequired")
- Given the same checkbox-level required CheckboxGroup, when the two required checkboxes are clicked one at a time to select them, then each becomes valid in turn and the group's aria-describedby is removed only once both are satisfied. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level isRequired")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with validationBehavior="native" and a group-level validate function requiring at least 3 selections, when rendered unchecked, then every checkbox reports invalid validity; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the custom message and focus moves to the first checkbox. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level validate function")
- Given the same group-level validate-function CheckboxGroup, when checkboxes are selected one at a time, then all three stay marked invalid until the third is checked, after which the group's aria-describedby is removed and every checkbox reports valid. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with validationBehavior="native" and per-checkbox validate functions on two of three Checkboxes, when rendered, then those two report invalid validity and the third reports valid; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group's aria-describedby lists both custom error messages and focus moves to the first invalid checkbox. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level validate function")
- Given the same checkbox-level validate-function CheckboxGroup, when each invalid checkbox is clicked in turn to select it, then its own custom error message is removed from the group's aria-describedby, and the aria-describedby disappears entirely once both are satisfied. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup named "terms" with validationBehavior="native" inside a Form whose submit handler sets server-side validationErrors, when the submit button is clicked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the server error message and every checkbox reports invalid validity; when the first checkbox is then clicked, then the group's aria-describedby is removed and every checkbox reports valid. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with isRequired, validationBehavior="native", and a custom errorMessage function keyed on validationDetails.valueMissing, when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the custom "Please select at least one item" message. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports customizing native error messages")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with the default validationBehavior="aria" and a group-level validate function requiring 3 selections, when rendered unchecked, then the group immediately has aria-describedby with the custom message and every checkbox has aria-invalid="true" while still reporting valid native validity. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level validate function")
- Given the same aria-validationBehavior group-level validate-function CheckboxGroup, when checkboxes are selected one at a time, then the group's aria-describedby persists until the third selection, after which it is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports group level validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with validationBehavior="aria" and per-checkbox validate functions on two of three Checkboxes, when rendered, then those two checkboxes have aria-invalid="true" (all still report native-valid) and the group's aria-describedby lists both custom messages. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level validate function")
- Given the same aria-validationBehavior checkbox-level validate-function CheckboxGroup, when the two invalid checkboxes are each clicked to select them, then the group's error message for each is removed in turn until the group's aria-describedby disappears entirely. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports checkbox level validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup named "terms" with the default validationBehavior="aria" inside a Form with server-side validationErrors, when rendered, then the group has aria-describedby with the server error message and every checkbox has aria-invalid="true"; when the first checkbox is clicked, then the group's aria-describedby is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with isRequired on both the group and its checkboxes and a matching className function, when inspected, then the group and each checkbox root carry data-required="true" and the required class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support required state")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered isRequired with a FieldError inside a native form, when rendered unchecked, then neither the group nor any checkbox is marked invalid; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with "Constraints not satisfied" and data-invalid, and every checkbox root gains data-invalid; when a checkbox is then clicked to select it, then all invalid markers are cleared. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support validation errors")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with a form="test" prop, when inspected, then every checkbox input carries a form attribute of "test". (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support form prop")
- Given a Spectrum 2 CheckboxGroup with no isRequired of its own nested inside a Form that has isRequired, when one checkbox is checked and the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group has no aria-describedby or data-invalid because a single selection satisfies the inherited requirement; when that checkbox is then unchecked and checkValidity is invoked again, then the group becomes invalid with data-invalid and aria-describedby pointing at a "Constraints not satisfied" message. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/CheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "should not require all checkboxes to be checked when Form has isRequired")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({name: 'awesome-react-aria'})`, when rendered, then every input gets that same `name`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "name can be controlled")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup({isRequired: true})` with `validationBehavior: 'native'` items, when an item is checked the group loses `data-invalid`, when it is unchecked again the group gains `data-invalid="true"`, and checking again clears it, so validation is live rather than only on submit. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "should re-validate in realtime")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup with a data-testid prop, when rendered, then the group element carries that attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "supports custom props")
- Given a Spectrum v3 CheckboxGroup where one child Checkbox is given isSelected, defaultSelected, or isEmphasized directly (three template variants), when rendered, then a console warning fires stating that the prop is unsupported on individual Checkbox elements within a CheckboxGroup and should be applied to the group instead. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/checkbox/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "warns if %s is passed to an individual checkbox")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup and its Checkbox/CheckboxField children rendered with no custom classes, when inspected, then the group has class "react-aria-CheckboxGroup" with aria-labelledby pointing at its Label text, and each checkbox root carries the default "react-aria-Checkbox" or "react-aria-CheckboxField" class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should render a checkbox group with default classes")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup and its Checkbox/CheckboxField children rendered with custom className strings on the group and on each checkbox, when inspected, then the group and each checkbox root use the custom classes instead of the defaults. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should render a checkbox group with custom classes")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with an arbitrary DOM prop (data-foo) on the group, when inspected, then the group element carries that attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support DOM props")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered with a custom render function supplying the group's root element, when inspected, then the custom root receives the computed props including a data-custom attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support custom render function")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered inside a CheckboxGroupContext.Provider that maps a named slot to extra props (an aria-label), when the group is given slot="test", then the group carries slot="test" and the mapped aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support slot")
- Given the RAC CheckboxGroup rendered isRequired with a render-prop function as children keyed on isRequired, when inspected, then the rendered Label element reflects data-required="true" supplied by the render prop. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/CheckboxGroup.test.js: "should support render props")
- Given `useCheckboxGroup` with an extra `data-testid` prop, when rendered, then the attribute passes through to the group element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/checkbox/useCheckboxGroup.test.tsx: "supports custom props")

## radio-group

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup/Radio pairing (template variant "Radio") with three radios and an onChange handler, when rendered, then it exposes role="radiogroup" containing three radios that all share the same generated name attribute and have values dogs/cats/dragons, all initially unchecked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name handles defaults")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup rendered with aria-label on the group and an aria-label on each Radio instead of visible Label text (template variant "Radio"), when rendered, then it still exposes role="radiogroup" with three radios sharing one generated name attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name renders without labels")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup/Radio pairing rendered inside a Provider with isDisabled (template variant "Radio"), when rendered, then every radio input carries the native disabled attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can be disabled via the Provider")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isReadOnly (template variant "Radio"), when rendered, then the group carries aria-readonly="true" while none of the individual radio inputs get an aria-readonly attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can be readonly")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with a label prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-labelledby pointing at an element whose text content is the label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup supports labeling")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with an aria-label prop, when rendered, then the group carries that aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup supports aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 Radio given an aria-label inside a labeled RadioGroup, when rendered, then that radio's input carries the aria-label. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 Radio supports aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with no orientation prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-orientation="vertical" by default. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup sets aria-orientation by default")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with orientation="horizontal", when rendered, then the group carries aria-orientation="horizontal". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup sets aria-orientation based on the orientation prop")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isInvalid, when rendered, then the group carries aria-invalid="true". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup sets aria-invalid when isInvalid")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isInvalid and an explicit aria-errormessage, when rendered, then the group carries aria-invalid="true" and the given aria-errormessage. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup passes through aria-errormessage")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isRequired, when rendered, then the group carries aria-required="true" while none of the individual radio inputs get aria-required. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup sets aria-required when isRequired is true")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isDisabled, when rendered, then the group carries aria-disabled="true". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "v3 RadioGroup sets aria-disabled when isDisabled is true")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with a description prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-describedby resolving to an element containing the description text. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "should support help text description")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with isReadOnly on the group and a matching className function on the radios, when inspected, then the group carries aria-readonly and the readonly class, and each radio root carries data-readonly and the readonly class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support read only state")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with isInvalid on the group and a matching className function, when inspected, then the group carries aria-invalid="true" and the invalid class, and each radio root carries data-invalid="true" and the invalid class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support invalid state")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with orientation="horizontal" and a className function keyed on orientation, when inspected, then the group carries aria-orientation="horizontal" and the horizontal class; when the RadioGroup tester then triggers the first radio by mouse and the third by keyboard, then each becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support orientation")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered invalid with a group-level description Text slot, an errorMessage Text slot, and a per-radio description, when inspected, then the group's aria-describedby resolves to "Description Error" and the first radio's aria-describedby resolves to its own description plus the group's error and description text, in an order that differs between the RadioField and plain Radio variants. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "supports help text")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with an explicit aria-describedby on each Radio child, when inspected, then every radio input carries that same aria-describedby value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support aria-describedby on a radio")

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with three radios rendered across eight template variants combining ltr/rtl locales, vertical/horizontal orientation, and left/right vs up/down arrow keys, when tabbing in (landing on the first radio) then pressing the arrow keys in sequence, then focus moves forward and backward through the radios and wraps at the ends, with left/right reversed under rtl while up/down stay direction-independent regardless of orientation. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name default keyboard navigation with wrapping")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with 3 horizontal radios where one or two are individually disabled (seven template variants covering middle/first/last/first-two disabled in both ltr and rtl locales), when tabbing in and repeatedly pressing ArrowRight/ArrowLeft, then the roving tabIndex skips the disabled radio(s) and wraps between only the enabled radios, regardless of locale direction. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name skips disabled radios")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with 4 horizontal radios where two adjacent radios are disabled (two template variants), when tabbing in and repeatedly pressing ArrowRight/ArrowLeft, then the roving tabIndex skips both disabled radios and wraps between the two enabled ones. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name skips multiple disabled radios")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with five radios (two individually disabled) rendered across ltr+vertical, rtl+vertical, ltr+horizontal, and rtl+horizontal locale/orientation combinations, when triggering radios by index, by keyboard interaction, and by label text with mouse interaction via the RadioGroup test-util tester, then each targeted radio becomes checked correctly regardless of orientation or RTL locale. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name should select the correct radio via keyboard regardless of orientation and disabled radios")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with a className function keyed on isPressed and onPress/onClick handlers, when tabbing to the first radio and pressing then releasing Space, then data-pressed and the pressed class toggle across the key press and onPress/onClick each fire once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support press state with keyboard")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered uncontrolled with onChange, when tabbing to the first radio and then dispatching a held-down ArrowDown key via low-level keydown/repeated-keydown/keyup events (bypassing userEvent's own radio-navigation shortcut), then the third radio ends up checked and onChange fires with 'c', confirming the component's own repeat-keydown handling works independent of the test library's built-in radio-group navigation. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support repeat keydown events when holding an arrow key")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with a className function keyed on isFocusVisible, when tabbing onto the group (landing on the first radio) and tabbing again, then focus leaves the group entirely instead of moving to the second radio; when shift-tabbing back into the group, then focus lands on the last radio, showing Tab treats the whole radiogroup as a single stop. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should not navigate within the group using Tab")
- Given a RAC RadioGroup where the first Radio contains a focusable non-radio child (an object element with tabIndex=-1), when tabbing in and pressing ArrowRight then ArrowLeft, then focus moves directly between the radio elements, skipping the embedded non-radio focusable content. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should navigate within the group using ArrowRight/Left but skip non-radios")
- Given a RAC RadioGroup with defaultValue="a" inside a native form with a submit button, when the first radio is clicked to focus it and Enter is pressed, then the form submits once via implicit submission. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support implicit form submission from a focused radio on Enter")
- Given a Spectrum 2 RadioGroup with five radios (two individually disabled) rendered across ltr+vertical, rtl+vertical, ltr+horizontal, and rtl+horizontal locale/orientation combinations, when triggering radios by index, by keyboard interaction, and by label text with mouse interaction via the RadioGroup test-util tester, then each targeted radio becomes checked and the group's aria-orientation matches the given orientation prop; when ArrowLeft is then pressed directly (bypassing the tester, to reproduce a past regression where orientation was not forwarded to the group), then the selection moves to the previous radio under ltr or vertical orientation, but under rtl+horizontal ArrowLeft instead selects the first radio because rtl reverses which arrow means "forward". (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/RadioGroup.test.tsx: "$Name should select the correct radio via keyboard regardless of orientation and disabled radios")
- Given a Spectrum 2 RadioGroup rendered in a real browser (vitest browser mode) with three described radios, when the RadioGroup test-util tester is configured for mouse interaction or for keyboard interaction (two template variants) and triggers the second radio, then the tester reports that radio as the selected radio in both interaction modes. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/RadioGroup.browser.test.tsx: "triggers a radio via $interactionType")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with onHoverStart/onHoverChange/onHoverEnd handlers and a className function keyed on isHovered, when hovering then unhovering the first radio's label, then data-hovered and the hover class toggle on and off and each hover callback fires the expected number of times. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support hover")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with a className function keyed on isPressed and onPress/onClick/onClickCapture handlers, when pressing then releasing the mouse button on the first radio's label, then data-pressed and the pressed class toggle across the press and each handler fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support press state")

### Focus management

- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup preceded by an unrelated Button, when tabbing through the page, then Tab moves focus from the button straight into the radio group (landing on the first radio) and treats the whole group as a single tab stop, wrapping past it back to the button rather than visiting each radio individually. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "does not tab through individual radios")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with no selection, when rendered, then all radios start with tabIndex="0"; when the second radio is clicked, then only that radio keeps tabIndex="0" and the others get tabIndex="-1", implementing roving tabindex tied to selection. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "RadioGroup default roving tabIndex")
- Given a controlled Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with surrounding buttons that programmatically change the selected value (using fake timers), when those buttons change the selection via props rather than direct radio interaction, then the roving tabIndex still updates after timers flush so only the newly selected radio ends up with tabIndex="0". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "RadioGroup roving tabIndex for controlled radios")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup where the middle Radio has autoFocus (using fake timers), when timers are flushed, then only the autofocused radio ends up with tabIndex="0" and the others get tabIndex="-1". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "RadioGroup roving tabIndex for autoFocus")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with a className function keyed on isFocusVisible, when tabbing onto then away from the first radio, then the label gains data-focus-visible and the focus class while keyboard-focused and loses both once focus moves on. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support focus ring")
- Given the same Tab-skipping RAC RadioGroup rendered inside a DialogTrigger's Modal/Dialog opened via a trigger button, when tabbing onto the group's first radio and tabbing again, then focus moves to the dialog's Close button rather than the group's other radios; when shift-tabbing back, then focus returns to the group's last radio, confirming the single-tab-stop behavior holds inside an overlay. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should not navigate within the group using Tab in Dialog")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with onFocus/onFocusChange/onBlur on the group, when tabbing onto the first radio, then onFocus/onFocusChange fire once; when ArrowRight then moves selection/focus to another radio inside the group, then onFocus/onFocusChange do not fire again; when tabbing out of the group, then onBlur fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support focus events")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with onFocus/onBlur on a single Radio, when its label is clicked twice in a row, then onFocus fires only on the first click and onBlur never fires between the two clicks. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should not trigger onBlur/onFocus on sequential presses of a Radio")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with onFocus/onBlur handlers on two different Radios, when the first radio's label is clicked then the second radio's label is clicked, then the first radio's onFocus fires once and its onBlur fires once as focus moves to the second radio, whose own onFocus also fires. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should trigger onBlur when moving focus between different Radio buttons")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with three radios and an onChange handler, when the Dogs radio is clicked, then onChange fires once with 'dogs' and only the Dogs radio becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name handles defaults")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with one individually isDisabled Radio, when the disabled radio's label is clicked, then onChange never fires and nothing becomes checked; when a different, enabled radio is then clicked, then onChange fires with its value and it becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can have a single disabled radio")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isReadOnly, when a radio is clicked, then onChange never fires. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can be readonly")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with defaultValue="dragons", when rendered, then Dragons starts checked; when Dogs is clicked, then onChange fires with 'dogs' and only Dogs becomes checked. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can have a default value")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup controlled with value="dragons", when Dogs is clicked, then onChange fires with 'dogs' but Dragons remains the checked radio because the controlled value prop has not changed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can be controlled")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup with defaultValue="a" and render-prop children on each Radio keyed on isSelected, when the second radio is clicked, then each radio's rendered text toggles between its plain label and a "(selected)" suffix to match the new selection. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support Radio render props")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with isDisabled on a single Radio and a matching className function, when inspected, then that radio's input is disabled and its label/root carry data-disabled="true" and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support disabled state on radio")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered with isDisabled on the group and a matching className function on both group and radio, when inspected, then the group carries aria-disabled and the disabled class, and every radio is disabled with data-disabled and the disabled class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support disabled state on group")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup rendered uncontrolled with onChange and a className function keyed on isSelected, when the RadioGroup tester triggers the first radio then the second, then onChange fires with 'a' then 'b', the tester's getSelectedRadio reflects each new selection, and data-selected/the selected class move to only the currently selected radio's label. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support selected state")

### Forms (name, value, validation, form reset)

- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with a name prop, when rendered, then every radio's input carries that name attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "$Name can be given a group name")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isInvalid and an errorMessage prop, when rendered, then the group carries aria-describedby resolving to an element containing the error message text. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "should support error message")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup controlled via React state with name="pet" inside a native form with a reset button, when a different radio is selected and the form is then reset, then the radios revert to the original selection (Dogs checked). (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports form reset")
- Given React 19's useActionState driving a form action prop (only run when React version is 19 or newer) with a RadioGroup bound to defaultValue from the action state, when the submit button runs the form action, then the checked radio changes from Dogs to Cats to reflect the updated defaultValue. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "resets to defaultValue when submitting form action")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isRequired and validationBehavior="native" inside a Form, when rendered unchecked, then every radio input has the required attribute (not aria-required) and reports invalid validity; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby referencing "Constraints not satisfied" and focus moves to the first radio; when that radio is then clicked, then every radio reports valid validity and the group's aria-describedby is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports isRequired")
- Given the same isRequired/validationBehavior="native" Spectrum v3 RadioGroup, when the form's checkValidity is invoked and then ArrowDown is pressed from the focused first radio, then the arrow-key selection satisfies the requirement, marking every radio valid and removing the group's aria-describedby, showing keyboard-driven selection also clears native validation state. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "updates validation state with the keyboard")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with validationBehavior="native", defaultValue="dragons", and a custom validate function that rejects "dragons", when rendered, then every radio reports invalid validity; when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the custom "Too scary" message and focus moves to the first radio; when a different radio is then clicked, then the group's aria-describedby is removed and every radio reports valid. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup named "pet" with validationBehavior="native" inside a Form whose submit handler sets server-side validationErrors, when the submit button is clicked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the server error message and every radio reports invalid validity; when a radio is then clicked, then the group's aria-describedby is removed and every radio reports valid. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with isRequired, validationBehavior="native", and a custom errorMessage function keyed on validationDetails.valueMissing, when the form's checkValidity is invoked, then the group gains aria-describedby with the custom "Please select a pet" message. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports customizing native error messages")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup with the default validationBehavior="aria", defaultValue="dragons", and a custom validate function that rejects "dragons", when rendered, then the group immediately has aria-describedby and aria-invalid="true" with the custom "Too scary" message while every radio still reports valid native validity; when a different radio is clicked, then the group's aria-describedby and aria-invalid are removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports validate function")
- Given a Spectrum v3 RadioGroup named "pet" with the default validationBehavior="aria" inside a Form with server-side validationErrors, when rendered, then the group has aria-describedby and aria-invalid="true" reflecting the server message; when a radio is then clicked, then both are removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/radio/Radio.test.js: "supports server validation")
- Given the RAC RadioGroup and its Radio/RadioField children rendered with isRequired on the group and a matching className function, when inspected, then the group carries aria-required="true" and the required class, and each radio root carries data-required="true" and the required class. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/RadioGroup.test.js: "should support required state")
