- Given a RAC `Modal isEntering`, when opened, then the `.react-aria-ModalOverlay` carries `data-entering`; removing the prop removes the attribute; with `isExiting` the overlay stays mounted with `data-exiting` after closing, and removing `isExiting` finally unmounts it. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Dialog.test.js: "supports isEntering and isExiting props")

### SSR and hydration

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Dialog aria-label="The label"`, when server-rendered and hydrated, then no errors occur. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/Dialog.ssr.test.js: "Dialog should render without errors")
- Given a RAC `DialogTrigger isOpen` with a `Modal`, `Dialog`, and autofocused `TextField` inside `React.StrictMode`, when server-rendered, then the HTML contains no dialog at all (overlays are client-only), and after hydration the dialog is in the document. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Dialog.ssr.test.js: "should render without errors")

### Accessibility tooling

- Given `useDialog` with no `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, or rendered title, when it mounts, then `console.warn` includes `A dialog must have a title for accessibility`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/dialog/useDialog.test.js: "should warn when dialog has no accessible title")
- Given `useDialog` with an `aria-label`, when it mounts, then no warning is logged. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/dialog/useDialog.test.js: "should not warn when aria-label is provided")
- Given `useDialog` with an `aria-labelledby`, when it mounts, then no warning is logged. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/dialog/useDialog.test.js: "should not warn when aria-labelledby is provided")
- Given `useDialog` whose child renders the returned `titleProps` on a heading, when it mounts, then no warning is logged. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/dialog/useDialog.test.js: "should not warn when a title element is rendered")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Dialog data-testid="test"` under a tray `DialogContext`, when rendered, then arbitrary data attributes pass through. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/Dialog.test.js: "should support custom data attributes")
- Given a RAC `Modal` inside an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` whose `getContainer` returns a custom div, when the dialog opens, then the dialog is rendered inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Dialog.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container provided by the PortalProvider")
- Given a RAC `Modal UNSTABLE_portalContainer={container}` (the deprecated prop), when the dialog opens, then the dialog is rendered inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Dialog.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container")

## alert-dialog

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 `AlertDialog` with a title and primary action, when rendered, then it exposes `role="alertdialog"` and `aria-describedby` resolves to the element containing the body content text. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "should have aria-describedby pointing to the content")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `AlertDialog aria-describedby="content-id"`, when rendered, then the author's value is used instead of a generated one. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "accepts custom aria-describedby")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a Spectrum v3 `AlertDialog` with only `primaryActionLabel` and `onPrimaryAction`, when the single button is clicked, then `onPrimaryAction` is called once with no arguments. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "renders alert dialog with onPrimaryAction")
- Given an `AlertDialog` with `primaryActionLabel` and `cancelLabel`, when cancel is clicked `onCancel` fires once and `onPrimaryAction` does not; when confirm is clicked afterwards `onPrimaryAction` fires once and `onCancel` is not called again. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "renders 2 button alert dialog with onPrimaryAction / onCancel")
- Given an `AlertDialog` with primary, secondary, and cancel labels, when each is clicked in turn, then only the matching callback fires each time and the others are untouched. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "renders a 3 button alert dialog with onPrimaryAction / onCancel")
- Given `AlertDialog isPrimaryActionDisabled`, when the confirm button is clicked, then `onPrimaryAction` is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "disable its confirm button")
- Given `AlertDialog isSecondaryActionDisabled`, when the secondary button is clicked, then no action callback fires. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "disable its secondary button")

### Focus management

- Given an `AlertDialog` with no `autoFocusButton`, when rendered, then the alert dialog element itself is focused. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "renders alert dialog with onPrimaryAction")
- Given `AlertDialog autoFocusButton="primary"`, when rendered, then the confirm button is the active element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "autofocus its confirm button")
- Given `AlertDialog autoFocusButton="cancel"`, when rendered, then the cancel button is the active element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "autofocus its cancel button")
- Given `AlertDialog autoFocusButton="secondary"`, when rendered, then the secondary button is the active element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "autofocus its secondary button")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given `AlertDialog data-testid="alert-dialog"` with all three action labels, when rendered, then the dialog gets the custom test id and the three buttons carry the stable test ids `rsp-AlertDialog-cancelButton`, `rsp-AlertDialog-secondaryButton`, and `rsp-AlertDialog-confirmButton`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/AlertDialog.test.js: "can add test ids")

## dialog-trigger

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 `DialogTrigger` with no `type`, when the trigger is pressed, then a dialog appears and a `modal` element is rendered, so `modal` is the default overlay type. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should trigger a modal by default")
- Given `DialogTrigger type="tray"`, when the trigger is pressed, then a dialog appears inside a `tray` element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should trigger a tray")
- Given `DialogTrigger type="popover"`, when the trigger is pressed, then a dialog appears inside a `popover` element that carries an inline `style` attribute from positioning. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should trigger a popover")
- Given `DialogTrigger` open with a modal, when the dialog is visible, then an ancestor of the root `Provider` carries `aria-hidden="true"`, and pressing Escape and waiting for the exit animation removes it. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should set aria-hidden on parent providers on mount and remove on unmount")

### Keyboard

- Given `DialogTrigger isKeyboardDismissDisabled`, when Escape is pressed with the dialog focused, then the dialog stays in the document and keeps focus; pressing the in-dialog close button still closes it. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "disable closing dialog via escape key")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given `DialogTrigger type="popover"` with a render-prop `close` wired to a Cancel button, when Cancel is clicked, then the dialog is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "popovers should be closeable")
- Given an open `DialogTrigger type="popover"`, when its trigger is clicked again, then the popover closes, focus returns to the trigger, and `onOpenChange` has been called twice in total. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "popovers should be closeable by clicking their trigger while they are open")
- Given nested `DialogTrigger type="popover"` layers where the inner dialog has a labelled text field, when the inner input is clicked, then it becomes the active element, so nested popovers stay interactive. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "input in nested popover should be interactive with a click")

### Focus management

- Given `DialogTrigger` rendered as each of `modal`, `popover`, and `tray` with two inputs in the dialog, when the trigger is pressed, then focus lands on the dialog element and Tab cycles input1 -> input2 -> input1, so focus is contained in all three overlay types. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "contains focus within the dialog when rendered as a %s")
- Given a modal `DialogTrigger`, when the dialog is closed and timers flush, then focus returns to the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should restore focus to the trigger when the dialog is closed")
- Given a popover `DialogTrigger`, when the hidden first dismiss button inside the popover is clicked and the exit animation completes, then focus returns to the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should restore focus to the trigger when the dialog is closed from a hidden dismiss button")
- Given nested modal `DialogTrigger`s each with an autofocused input, when the inner dialog opens, then focus is on the inner input; clicking the body or programmatically focusing an input outside both dialogs leaves focus on the inner input, so the inner scope never hands focus back to the outer dialog. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should not try to restore focus to the outer dialog when the inner dialog opens")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given `DialogTrigger isOpen={false} onOpenChange`, when the trigger is pressed, then no dialog appears but `onOpenChange(true)` fires; re-rendering with `isOpen` shows the dialog; Escape leaves the dialog mounted while calling `onOpenChange(false)`; only re-rendering with `isOpen={false}` unmounts it. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "can be controlled")
- Given `DialogTrigger defaultOpen`, when rendered, then the dialog is visible, and Escape calls `onOpenChange(false)` once and removes the dialog after the exit animation. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "can be uncontrolled with defaultOpen")
- Given `DialogTrigger defaultOpen` with a render-prop `close` wired to a user-added button, when that button is pressed, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires once and the dialog unmounts after the animation. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "can be closed by buttons the user adds")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given `DialogTrigger isDismissable defaultOpen`, when the `Dismiss` labelled button is clicked, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires once and the dialog unmounts after the animation. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "can be closed by dismiss button in dialog")
- Given `DialogTrigger type="modal" isDismissable defaultOpen`, when `document.body` is clicked, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires once and the dialog unmounts. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "dismissable modals can be closed by clicking outside the dialog")
- Given `DialogTrigger type="modal" defaultOpen` without `isDismissable`, when `document.body` is clicked, then the dialog stays visible and `onOpenChange` is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "non dismissable modals cannot be closed by clicking outside the dialog")
- Given `DialogTrigger type="popover" isDismissable={false} defaultOpen`, when `document.body` is clicked, then the dialog still closes, so non-modal overlays ignore `isDismissable` for outside interaction. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "non-modals can be closed by clicking outside the dialog regardless of isDismissable")

### RTL and i18n

- Given `simulateMobile()` and `DialogTrigger type="popover"`, when the trigger is pressed, then a `modal` is rendered instead of a popover, so popovers degrade to modals on small screens. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should trigger a modal instead of a popover on mobile")
- Given `simulateMobile()` and `DialogTrigger type="popover" mobileType="tray"`, when the trigger is pressed, then a `tray` is rendered. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should trigger a tray instead of a popover on mobile if mobileType=\"tray\"")
- Given `simulateMobile()` and `DialogTrigger type="popover" mobileType="modal" defaultOpen`, when `document.body` is clicked, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires and the modal unmounts, so the mobile fallback modal stays dismissable. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "mobile type modals should be closable by clicking outside the modal")

### Accessibility tooling

- Given a `DialogTrigger` nested inside a `MenuTrigger`'s menu item, so that activating the item unmounts the menu (and therefore the trigger) while the dialog opens, when the item is clicked, then the menu, menu item, and dialog are all gone and `console.warn` is called once with `A DialogTrigger unmounted while open. This is likely due to being placed within a trigger that unmounts or inside a conditional. Consider using a DialogContainer instead.`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should warn when unmounting a dialog trigger while a modal is open")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a `DialogTrigger` wrapped in an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` returning a custom container, when the modal opens, then the dialog is rendered inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container")
- Given the same portal provider with `type="tray"`, when opened, then the tray's dialog is rendered inside the custom container. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should render the tray in the portal container")
- Given the same portal provider with `type="popover"`, when opened, then the popover's dialog is rendered inside the custom container. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogTrigger.test.js: "should render the popover in the portal container")

## dialog-container

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 `DialogContainer`, when Escape is pressed with the dialog focused, then the dialog closes. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should support closing a dialog via the Escape key")
- Given `DialogContainer isKeyboardDismissDisabled`, when Escape is pressed, then the dialog stays visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should not close a dialog via the Escape key if isKeyboardDismissDisabled")

### Focus management

- Given a `DialogContainer` opened from a button, when it opens, then focus is on the dialog, and pressing the in-dialog Confirm button (which calls `useDialogContainer().dismiss`) closes it. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should open and close a dialog based on controlled state")
- Given nested `DialogContainer`s opened from a menu item, when the first dialog's action opens a second dialog, then focus lands on the second dialog's confirm button; dismissing it returns focus all the way back to the original trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should be able to have dialogs open dialogs and still restore focus")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given a `DialogContainer` with no `isDismissable`, when `document.body` is clicked, then the dialog stays visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should not close when clicking outside the dialog by default")
- Given `DialogContainer isDismissable`, when `document.body` is clicked, then the dialog closes. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should close when clicking outside the dialog when isDismissible")
- Given a `DialogContainer` whose dialog is opened from a menu item (so the menu and item unmount), when the item is clicked, then the menu disappears but the dialog stays open and can still be confirmed. This is the documented fix for the `DialogTrigger` unmount warning. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should not close the dialog when a trigger unmounts")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a `DialogContainer` wrapped in an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` returning a custom container, when opened, then the dialog renders inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/dialog/DialogContainer.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container")

## modal

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Modal isOpen`, when Escape is pressed on the dialog inside it, then `onOpenChange(false)` is called once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Modal.test.js: "hides the modal when pressing the escape key")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Modal` with no `isOpen`, when rendered, then nothing is in the document and `document.documentElement` has no `overflow: hidden`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Modal.test.js: "should render nothing if isOpen is not set")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Modal isOpen`, when the enter animation finishes, then the dialog is visible and `document.documentElement` gets `overflow: hidden`, locking body scroll. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Modal.test.js: "should render when isOpen is true")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Modal isOpen` without `isDismissable`, when a mouseup fires on the body, then `onOpenChange` is never called. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Modal.test.js: "doesn't hide the modal when clicking outside by default")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Modal isOpen isDismissable`, when mousedown and mouseup fire on the body, then `onOpenChange` is called once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Modal.test.js: "hides the modal when clicking outside if isDismissible is true")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Overlay` with no `isOpen`, when rendered, then the overlay ref stays `null`, so nothing is mounted. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Overlay.test.js: "should render nothing if isOpen is not set")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Overlay isOpen`, when rendered, then its DOM node's parent is `document.body` and it is not inside the `Provider`, confirming it portals to the body. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Overlay.test.js: "should render into a portal in the body")
- Given a React Aria Components `ModalOverlay isDismissable` that scrolls, containing a tall `Modal` offset 5dvh from the top, when a trusted mousedown is held on the backdrop in a real browser, then the overlay's `scrollTop` stays 0 and the modal's viewport top does not change after two animation frames, so FocusScope's focus restore does not scroll the modal into view. Pins a Chrome and Safari behavior where mousedown on the backdrop moves focus to `<body>` (Firefox does not), which cannot be reproduced in jsdom or Chromatic. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Modal.browser.test.tsx: "does not scroll the modal into view when the backdrop is pressed")

## tray

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tray isOpen`, when rendered, then two buttons with `aria-label="Dismiss"` wrap the content (one before, one after) for screen-reader users, and clicking the first calls `onOpenChange` once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "should have hidden dismiss buttons for screen readers")

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tray isOpen`, when Escape is pressed on the dialog, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "hides the tray when pressing the escape key")

### Focus management

- Given `Tray isOpen shouldCloseOnBlur` with a button outside it, when focus moves to the outside button, then `onOpenChange` is called once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "hides the tray on blur when shouldCloseOnBlur is true")
- Given `Tray isOpen shouldCloseOnBlur` with nothing to receive focus, when the dialog is blurred with no replacement (the tab-switch case), then `onOpenChange` is not called and the tray stays visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "does not close when focus is lost with no replacement, such as switching tabs")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tray` with no `isOpen`, when rendered, then nothing is mounted and no `overflow: hidden` is applied. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "should render nothing if isOpen is not set")
- Given `Tray isOpen`, when rendered, then the dialog is visible and `document.documentElement` gets `overflow: hidden`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "should render when isOpen is true")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given `Tray isOpen`, when mousedown and mouseup fire on the body, then `onOpenChange` fires once, so trays always dismiss on outside interaction. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Tray.test.js: "hides the tray when clicking outside")

## popover

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover` wrapping a `Dialog`, when the enter animation completes, then the dialog has `tabIndex="-1"`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "$Name has a tabIndex set")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover isOpen`, when rendered, then two `aria-label="Dismiss"` buttons wrap the content for screen readers, and clicking the first calls `onOpenChange` once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "should have hidden dismiss buttons for screen readers")
- Given a React Aria Components `DialogTrigger` with a `Popover` and `Dialog`, when opened, then the `.react-aria-Popover` element carries `data-trigger="DialogTrigger"`, the trigger button gets `aria-controls`, and the dialog's `id` matches that `aria-controls` value. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "works with a dialog")
- Given a RAC `Popover aria-label="Popover"` used without an inner `Dialog`, when opened, then the popover itself takes the dialog role and its id matches the trigger's `aria-controls`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "applies overlay id to standalone popover")

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover isOpen`, when Escape is pressed on the popover, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "hides the popover when pressing the escape key")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover` around a `Dialog` with two inputs, when Tab is pressed repeatedly, then focus cycles dialog -> input1 -> input2 -> input1, so focus is contained. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "$Name contains focus within the popover")

### Focus management

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover` around a `Dialog` with two plain inputs, when it opens, then focus goes to the dialog element rather than the first input. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "$Name auto focuses the first tabbable element by default")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover isOpen` around a `Dialog` with no focusable child, when it opens, then the dialog itself is focused. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "$Name auto focuses the dialog itself if there is no focusable child")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover` whose second input has `autoFocus`, when it opens, then that input is focused. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "$Name allows autofocus prop on a child element to work as expected")
- Given `Popover isOpen shouldCloseOnBlur` containing an autofocused button, with another button outside, when focus moves to the outside button, then `onOpenChange` fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "hides the popover on blur when shouldCloseOnBlur is true")
- Given `Popover isOpen shouldCloseOnBlur`, when the focused button inside is blurred with no replacement (the tab-switch case), then `onOpenChange` is not called and the popover stays visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "does not close when focus is lost with no replacement, such as switching tabs")
- Given a RAC `DialogTrigger` with a `Popover`, when the trigger is tabbed to and pressed the dialog takes focus, and when the body is clicked and timers flush focus returns to the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should handle focus")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a RAC `Popover isOpen triggerRef` used standalone with no `DialogTrigger`, when mousedown and mouseup fire on the body, then `onOpenChange(false)` fires exactly once. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should support being used standalone")
- Given a RAC `Popover isOpen` nested inside a `DialogTrigger`, when the body is pressed, then the popover's own `onOpenChange(false)` fires once, so its own props override the trigger's context state. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "isOpen and defaultOpen should override state from context")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Popover isOpen`, when the body is clicked, then `onOpenChange` fires once. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/overlays/Popover.test.js: "hides the popover when clicking outside")
- Given a RAC popover dialog, when the body is clicked, then the dialog is removed from the document. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "works with a dialog")

### Positioning

- Given a RAC `Popover placement="bottom start"` with a function child, when opened, then the render prop receives `placement` as `bottom`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should support render props")
- Given a RAC `Popover style={{zIndex: 5}}` and an `OverlayArrow style={{top: 5}}`, when opened, then the computed positioning styles merge with the author styles so `z-index: 5` survives on the popover and `top: 5px` on the arrow. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "supports overriding styles")

### Animation and presence

- Given a RAC `Popover isEntering`, when opened, then `.react-aria-Popover` has `data-entering`; removing the prop removes it; with `isExiting` the popover stays mounted with `data-exiting` after closing, and removing `isExiting` finally unmounts it. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "supports isEntering and isExiting props")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a RAC `Popover render={props => <div {...props} data-custom="true" />}`, when opened, then the custom render function's props land on the popover element that wraps the dialog. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should support custom render function")
- Given a RAC `Popover` inside an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` returning a custom container, when opened, then the dialog renders inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container set by the PortalProvider")
- Given a RAC `Popover UNSTABLE_portalContainer={container}` (the deprecated prop), when opened, then the dialog renders inside that container. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should render the dialog in the portal container")
- Given a RAC `DialogTrigger` whose trigger is a `Pressable` wrapping a `<span role="button">`, when the span is clicked, then the popover dialog opens, so an arbitrary custom trigger element works. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Popover.test.js: "should support custom Pressable trigger")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ContextualHelp` with a heading, when rendered, then a quiet action button (class `spectrum-ActionButton--quiet`) is shown and no dialog exists yet. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "renders quiet action button")
- Given a `ContextualHelp`, when the button is clicked, then a dialog appears inside a `popover` element and the heading text is visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "opens a popover")
- Given a `ContextualHelp` with a `Content` child, when opened, then the content text is visible in the dialog. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "renders content")
- Given a `ContextualHelp` with a `Footer` containing a `Link`, when opened, then the link is visible and its parent has class `react-spectrum-ContextualHelp-footer`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "renders a link")
- Given a `ContextualHelp` with the default `help` variant, when rendered, then the trigger's `aria-label` is `Help`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "includes a default aria-label")
- Given `ContextualHelp variant="info"`, when rendered, then the trigger's `aria-label` is `Information`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "includes a default aria-label for info variant")
- Given `ContextualHelp aria-label="test"`, when rendered, then the custom label overrides the localized default. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "supports a custom aria-label")
- Given `ContextualHelp aria-labelledby="test"`, when rendered, then the trigger has `aria-labelledby` and no `aria-label`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/contextualhelp/ContextualHelp.test.js: "supports a custom aria-labelledby")

## preview-trigger

### Roles and attributes

- Given a RAC `PreviewTrigger` around a link, when closed, then the link has `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded="false"`, and no `aria-controls`; when opened by keyboard focus, `aria-expanded` becomes `"true"` and `aria-controls` points at the preview popover's id. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "exposes aria-haspopup, aria-expanded, and aria-controls on the trigger")
- Given a `PreviewTrigger` opened by keyboard focus, when the preview appears, then the link's `aria-describedby` contains the preview's id. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "opens on keyboard focus")

### Keyboard

- Given an open preview with focus inside it, when Escape is pressed, then the preview is removed and focus returns to the link. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "closes on Escape and restores focus to the link")
- Given `PreviewTrigger delay={300}`, when the link receives keyboard focus, then the preview is not yet open, and only after 300ms elapses does it appear, unlike a tooltip which opens on focus immediately. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "delays opening on keyboard focus")
- Given `PreviewTrigger delay={300}`, when focus moves on to the next element after only 150ms, then the preview never opens even after all timers run. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "does not open when tabbing through before the delay elapses")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a `PreviewTrigger` with pointer events installed and mocked rects placing the popover below the link with a gap, when the pointer hovers the link the preview opens; when the pointer moves into the gap between link and popover it stays open (the safe area); when it moves over the popover it stays open; when it moves far outside both it closes even with `closeDelay` of 0. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "opens on hover, stays open across the safe area, and closes when the pointer leaves")
- Given a `PreviewTrigger` with pointer events, when a touch pointerdown on the link is held past the 500ms long-press threshold, then the preview opens, gets `tabindex="-1"`, and receives focus itself so touch screen readers move into it. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "opens on long press and moves focus into the popover")
- Given a touch-capable window (`window.ontouchstart` defined), when the modality is keyboard, then the text `Long press to open preview` is absent; after a touch pointerdown plus mousedown switch the modality to pointer, then that hint appears and the link's `aria-describedby` includes its id. Pins the touch-only long-press hint. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "only describes the long press interaction when using touch")
- Given `PreviewTrigger isDisabled`, when the link is hovered, then no preview appears. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "does not open when disabled")

### Focus management

- Given an open preview, when Tab is pressed from the link, then focus moves into the preview's `Action` button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "moves focus into the preview when tabbing from the link")
- Given focus moved into the preview, when timers flush, then the preview is still in the document and the `Action` button keeps focus. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "stays open while focus is inside the preview")
- Given focus on the last element inside the preview, when Tab is pressed, then focus moves to the element after the trigger link in the document, so the popover's FocusScope maps tab-out relative to the trigger. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "tabs out of the preview to the element after the trigger")
- Given an open preview with focus inside and keyboard modality, when the hidden `Dismiss` button (used by screen readers) is clicked via `fireEvent.click` so modality stays keyboard, then the preview closes, focus is restored to the link, and the preview does not reopen from that restored focus. Handled via the `react-aria-focus-scope-restore` event. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "does not reopen when closed via the popover Dismiss button (focus restore)")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given `PreviewTrigger isOpen onOpenChange`, when rendered, then the preview is already in the document. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "supports controlled open state")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given a `PreviewTrigger` opened by keyboard focus, when rendered, then no underlay element exists, so the preview popover is non-modal. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/PreviewTrigger.test.js: "is non-modal (no underlay)")

## s2-dialog

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum 2 `AlertDialog title="Test" primaryActionLabel="Test"` with plain text children opened from a `DialogTrigger`, when opened, then it has `role="alertdialog"` and its `aria-describedby` resolves to an element containing the body text. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/AlertDialog.test.tsx: "automatically links to the content with aria-describedby")
- Given a Spectrum 2 `AlertDialog aria-describedby="content-id"` with two paragraphs in `Content`, when opened, then `aria-describedby` resolves to the specific paragraph the author pointed at rather than the whole content. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/AlertDialog.test.tsx: "accepts custom aria-describedby")
- Given a Spectrum 2 standard `Dialog` with `Heading slot="title"`, `Header`, `Content`, `Footer`, and a `ButtonGroup`, when opened, then it has `role="dialog"` and no `aria-describedby` at all, so only alert dialogs auto-describe. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/StandardDialog.test.tsx: "does not automatically add aria-describedby")
- Given a Spectrum 2 `Dialog aria-describedby="content-id"`, when opened, then `aria-describedby` resolves to the named paragraph. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/StandardDialog.test.tsx: "accepts custom aria-describedby")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a Spectrum 2 `CustomDialog isDismissible` containing a `TagGroup` with `maxRows={1}` and `onRemove`, when opened, then the dialog is visible, so a collection component that measures itself works inside a custom dialog. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/CustomDialog.test.tsx: "should allow you to render a taggroup inside")

## coach-mark

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a Spectrum 2 `CoachMarkTrigger isOpen` around a checkbox with a `CoachMark placement="right top"` containing a card preview, title, action menu, keyboard hint, description, step counter, and Previous/Next buttons (with `Element.prototype.animate` mocked), when timers flush, then exactly four buttons are present (two hidden Dismiss buttons plus the two action buttons), and clicking the third one fires its `onPress`. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/CoachMark.test.tsx: "renders a coachmark")

## tooltip

### Roles and attributes

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tooltip` rendered with children, when queried by role, then it exposes role="tooltip" and its text content matches the children. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/Tooltip.test.js: "supports children")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tooltip` rendered with an `aria-label` prop, when queried by role, then the tooltip element carries that `aria-label` attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/Tooltip.test.js: "supports aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tooltip` rendered with an `aria-labelledby` prop, when queried by role, then the tooltip element carries that `aria-labelledby` attribute. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/Tooltip.test.js: "supports aria-labelledby")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` in the "accessibility" describe block, when the trigger has not been hovered, then it has no `aria-describedby` attribute; when hovered so the tooltip opens, then the trigger gains `aria-describedby` pointing at the tooltip's id; when the pointer leaves and timers run out, then the `aria-describedby` attribute is removed again. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "has a trigger described by the tooltip when open")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger`/`Tooltip` with `delay={0}`, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then the trigger gains `aria-describedby` pointing at the tooltip id, the tooltip has class `react-aria-Tooltip`, and a `data-test` prop passed to `Tooltip` is forwarded to the rendered element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "shows on hover")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger`/`Tooltip` with `delay={0}`, when the trigger is given keyboard focus via tab, then the trigger gains `aria-describedby` pointing at the tooltip id and the tooltip has class `react-aria-Tooltip`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "shows on focus")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` whose `Tooltip` is passed a custom `render` prop, when the trigger is focused so the tooltip opens, then the rendered element is produced by the custom render function and carries the `data-custom` attribute it set. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should support custom render function")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` wrapping a `Button` with `isPending`, when the button is hovered, then the tooltip still opens and shows its text content despite the button's pending state. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "shows on hover when button has isPending")

### Keyboard

- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` opened via keyboard focus, when Escape is pressed and released on the active element, then `onOpenChange` fires with false and the tooltip is removed from the document after the close delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be keyboard force closed")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` opened via hover while a separate text input holds focus, when Escape is pressed and released on the currently focused element (the input, not the trigger), then the tooltip still closes. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be keyboard force closed from anywhere")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` opened via keyboard focus, when Enter is pressed and released on the trigger button, then `onOpenChange` fires with false and the tooltip closes after the close delay, matching mouse-click dismissal. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "is closed if the trigger is clicked with the keyboard")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` and `shouldCloseOnPress={false}` opened via keyboard focus, when Enter is pressed and released on the trigger, then `onOpenChange` is not called again and the tooltip stays visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "does not close if the trigger is clicked with the keyboard when shouldCloseOnPress is false")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` (immediate describe block), when the trigger is hovered, then the tooltip opens immediately and `onOpenChange` fires with true; when the pointer unhovers, then the tooltip stays visible until the close delay elapses and then `onOpenChange` fires with false and it is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens for hover")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` that is both focused and then hovered, when the pointer subsequently unhovers, then the tooltip closes even though the trigger remains focused. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "if hovered and focused, will hide if hover leaves")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` opened via hover, when the trigger is clicked, then `onOpenChange` fires with false and the tooltip is removed after the close delay. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "is closed if the trigger is clicked")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` and `shouldCloseOnPress={false}` opened via hover, when the trigger is clicked, then `onOpenChange` is not called again and the tooltip remains visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "does not close if the trigger is clicked when shouldCloseOnPress is false")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` using the default warmup delay (1500ms), when the trigger is hovered, then the tooltip does not appear at the halfway point of the delay and only becomes visible once the full delay elapses, after which unhover keeps it visible through one close-time interval before it closes on the second. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens for hover")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` using the default warmup delay, when the pointer hovers then leaves before half the delay has elapsed, then `onOpenChange` is never called and no tooltip ever appears even after all timers run out. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "never opens if blurred before it opens")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with a custom `delay={350}`, when the trigger is hovered, then the tooltip does not appear until the custom 350ms delay (not the default 1500ms) fully elapses, and unhover closes it after the close delay. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens for hover")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `trigger="focus"`, when the trigger is hovered (mouseenter/mousemove), then no tooltip ever opens. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "will not open for hover")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` whose trigger child is wrapped in `Focusable` (a plain `span role="button"`), when that span is hovered, then the tooltip still opens. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should support custom Focusable trigger on hover")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` whose trigger child is wrapped in `Pressable` (a plain `span role="button"`), when that span is hovered, then the tooltip still opens. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should support custom Pressable trigger")
- Given `useTooltipTrigger` plus `useTooltip` with the default delay and pointer modality established by a `mousemove`, when the trigger is entered with the mouse, then no `role="tooltip"` exists until the timers run, and after they run the tooltip appears. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "opens tooltip on hover after delay")
- Given `useTooltipTriggerState({delay: 0})`, when the trigger is entered with the mouse, then the tooltip is present immediately with no timer advance. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "opens tooltip immediately on hover with `delay: 0`")
- Given an open zero-delay tooltip, when the mouse leaves the trigger and timers run, then the tooltip is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "hides tooltip when hover leaves the trigger")
- Given an open tooltip whose props were produced by `useTooltip(tooltipProps, state)` (so the tooltip itself is hoverable), when the mouse leaves the trigger and enters the tooltip, then the tooltip stays present even after all timers run. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "keeps tooltip open when it gets hovered")
- Given a hoverable tooltip the pointer has moved into, when the mouse then leaves the tooltip and timers run, then the tooltip is removed. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "hides tooltip when hover leaves")
- Given a hoverable tooltip the pointer has moved into, when the mouse leaves the tooltip and returns to the trigger, then the tooltip stays present after all timers run. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/tooltip/useTooltip.test.js: "keeps tooltip open when hover returns to trigger from the tooltip")

### Focus management

- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` (immediate describe block), when the trigger is focused, then `onOpenChange` fires with true and the tooltip is visible; when the trigger is blurred, then `onOpenChange` fires with false and the tooltip is removed once the close delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens for focus")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` that is focused then also hovered, when focus is subsequently removed via blur, then the tooltip closes immediately even though the pointer is still hovering, and the later unhover causes no further `onOpenChange` calls. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "if hovered and focused, will hide if focus leaves")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` using the default warmup delay, when the trigger receives keyboard focus, then the tooltip opens immediately regardless of the hover delay, and closes after the close delay once blurred. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens immediately for focus")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` using the default warmup delay where a hover has already started the open-delay timer halfway, when the trigger receives keyboard focus mid-delay, then the tooltip opens immediately, stays visible once the original hover delay would have elapsed, and closes normally once blurred and unhovered. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens for focus even if the delay was already in process")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with a custom `delay={350}`, when the trigger receives keyboard focus, then the tooltip opens immediately without waiting for the custom delay. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "opens immediately for focus")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` and `trigger="focus"`, when the trigger is focused, then the tooltip opens and stays visible through a subsequent hover-then-unhover cycle, closing only once the trigger is blurred and the close delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "will open for focus")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` whose trigger child is wrapped in `Focusable` (a plain `span role="button"`), when tab is pressed, then focus moves to that span (tabindex 0) and the tooltip opens. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should support custom Focusable trigger on focus")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` in the "multiple tooltips" describe block with two triggers each at `delay={0}`, when one trigger is focused and then the other is focused after blurring the first, then only the currently-focused trigger's tooltip is visible at any time and the other trigger's tooltip text cannot be found. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can only show one tooltip at a time")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `isOpen` and `delay={0}` (controlled), when the trigger is focused and then blurred, then `onOpenChange` is never called and the tooltip remains visible throughout because the open state is controlled externally. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be controlled open")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `isOpen={false}` and `delay={0}` (controlled), when the trigger is focused, then `onOpenChange` fires with true but the tooltip never renders because the controlled open state stays false. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be controlled hidden")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `defaultOpen` and `delay={0}` (uncontrolled), when first rendered, then the tooltip is already visible; when the trigger is then blurred, then it closes after the close delay like a normal uncontrolled open. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be uncontrolled open")
- Given two uncontrolled Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger`s each at `delay={0}`, when the first is opened via focus and then the second is opened via hover, then the first tooltip's text disappears and only the second's is visible, enforcing a single open tooltip at a time. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "does not allow more than one tooltip open at a time")
- Given one controlled (`isOpen`) and one uncontrolled Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger`, each at `delay={0}`, when the uncontrolled trigger is opened via hover while the controlled one is already open, then both tooltips are visible simultaneously, exempting the controlled tooltip from the single-open-tooltip rule. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "two can be open at once if in controlled open state")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `isDisabled` and `delay={0}`, when the trigger is hovered, then no tooltip role is ever found in the document. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be disabled from the TooltipTrigger")
- Given the bare `useTooltipTriggerState`/`useTooltipTrigger` hooks with a custom `closeDelay={100}` (shorter than the default 500ms cooldown), when the trigger is hovered until it opens (after the default 1500ms open delay) and then unhovered, then the tooltip stays open through half of the custom close delay and `onOpenChange` fires with false only once the full 100ms custom close delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "closes with a custom close delay less than the default")
- Given the bare hooks with `closeDelay={650}` and a custom open `delay={350}` (close delay longer than the default 500ms cooldown), when the trigger is hovered until it opens and then unhovered, then the tooltip is still open and `onOpenChange` has not fired false after the default 500ms cooldown window passes, closing only once the remaining time up to the full 650ms close delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "closes with a custom close delay more than the default")
- Given two independently controlled (each via local `onOpenChange`-backed state) bare-hook `TooltipTrigger`s, when the first is hovered open and then the second is hovered open, then opening the second causes the first's `onOpenChange` to be called a second time with false, closing the previous tooltip. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "closes previus tooltip when opening a new one")

### Dismissal and layering

- Given ten Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger`s stacked inside a scrollable container, when one trigger's tooltip is opened via focus and then the container fires a scroll event, then the tooltip becomes not visible. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "should hide tooltip on scroll")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` nested inside an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` whose `getContainer` points at a custom DOM node, when the trigger is focused so the tooltip opens, then the tooltip's content renders inside that custom container element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "should render the tooltip in the portal container")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` inside a nested `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` with `getContainer={null}` inside an outer `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` pointing at a custom container, when the trigger is focused so the tooltip opens, then the tooltip's content does not render inside the outer custom container, because the inner provider's null override takes precedence. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "should no longer render the tooltip in the portal container")
- Given ten React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger`s each at `delay={0}` stacked inside a scrollable container, when one trigger's tooltip is opened via hover and then the container fires a scroll event, then the tooltip becomes not visible. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should hide tooltip on scroll")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` rendered inside an `UNSAFE_PortalProvider` whose `getContainer` points at a custom DOM node, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then the tooltip renders inside that custom container element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should render the tooltip in the portal container provided by the PortalProvider")
- Given a React Aria Components `Tooltip` using the deprecated `UNSTABLE_portalContainer` prop pointed at a custom DOM node, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then the tooltip renders inside that custom container. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should render the tooltip in the portal container")

### Positioning

- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger`/`Tooltip` with `delay={0}` and an `OverlayArrow` child, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then the tooltip has `data-placement="top"` and inline `position: absolute` styling, and the arrow element also has `position: absolute` styling. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "shows on hover")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger`/`Tooltip` with `delay={0}` and an `OverlayArrow` child, when the trigger is given keyboard focus, then the tooltip has `data-placement="top"` and inline `position: absolute` styling, and the arrow also has `position: absolute` styling. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "shows on focus")
- Given a React Aria Components `Tooltip` with `placement="bottom start"` whose children is a render-prop function, when the trigger is focused so the tooltip opens, then the render prop receives `placement` resolved to `"bottom"` and the tooltip text reflects it. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should support render props")

### Animation and presence

- Given a React Aria Components `Tooltip` rendered with `isEntering`, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then it carries `data-entering`; when re-rendered without `isEntering`, then `data-entering` is removed; when re-rendered with `isExiting` and the trigger is unhovered, then the tooltip stays in the document with `data-exiting` until re-rendered without `isExiting`, at which point it is removed from the document. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "supports isEntering and isExiting props")
- Given the bare hooks exposing `state.shouldSkipAnimation` (surfaced here as `data-instant`) across two adjacent triggers, when the first trigger is hovered and warms up through the full open delay, then it opens with `data-instant="false"` (animated in); when the pointer then moves directly to the second trigger while the first is still warm, then the first tooltip closes instantly and the second opens instantly, both flagged `data-instant="true"` (no animation); when the pointer finally leaves the second trigger entirely, then it closes with `data-instant="false"` (animated out) after the cooldown period elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "animates the first, swaps subsequent instantly, and animates out the last")

### Notes and edge cases

- Given a Spectrum v3 `Tooltip` rendered with a ref, when the ref is dereferenced via `UNSAFE_getDOMNode`, then it returns the same DOM node found by role="tooltip". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/Tooltip.test.js: "supports a ref")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` wrapping a `Tooltip` with a ref, when the trigger is focused so the tooltip renders, then the ref's `UNSAFE_getDOMNode()` returns the same node found by role="tooltip". (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "supports a ref on the Tooltip")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` using the default warmup delay, once a tooltip has fully opened and then closed via mouse-leave and Escape, then a subsequent hover reopens it instantly (skipping the warmup delay) for a limited "warm" period shared globally; but once that warm period is allowed to run out completely via `jest.runAllTimers`, then the next hover must again wait through the full open delay before the tooltip reappears. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "once opened, it can be closed and opened instantly for a period of time")
- Given two separate Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger`s using the default warmup delay, when the first trigger is hovered only through half the open delay and then left, then no tooltip appears for either trigger; when the second trigger is then hovered, then its own open-delay warmup starts fresh from zero (does not inherit the first trigger's partial progress) and only shows its tooltip once its own full delay elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "will not show a tooltip if the trigger is left during warmup, nor will warmup complete")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `TooltipTrigger` with `delay={0}` wrapping an `ActionButton` that itself has `isDisabled` (rather than `isDisabled` on the `TooltipTrigger`), when the trigger is hovered, then the tooltip still does not open, despite the test file's own inline comment questioning whether disabling the inner button rather than the trigger should really suppress the tooltip this way. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/tooltip/TooltipTrigger.test.js: "can be disabled from the trigger")
- Given a React Aria Components `Tooltip` given a custom `style={{zIndex: 5}}` prop, when the trigger is hovered so the tooltip opens, then the resulting inline style string includes `z-index: 5` merged alongside the library's own positioning styles. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "supports overriding styles")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` wrapping a `Button` with `excludeFromTabOrder`, when rendered, then the button keeps `tabindex="-1"` rather than having the tooltip trigger's own tab-order handling override it. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should not override child properties for excludeFromTabOrder")
- Given a React Aria Components `TooltipTrigger` wrapping a plain `Button`, when rendered, then the button keeps its default `tabindex="0"`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Tooltip.test.js: "should not override child properties")
- Given the bare hooks with a negative `closeDelay={-50}`, when the trigger is hovered until it opens and then unhovered, then `onOpenChange` fires with false immediately with no close delay. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "closes immediately with a close delay less than zero")
- Given the bare hooks with `closeDelay={0}`, when the trigger is hovered until it opens and then unhovered, then `onOpenChange` fires with false immediately with no close delay. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "closes immediately with a close delay of zero")
- Given the bare hooks driven by a component that exposes manual `state.open(false)`/`state.close(true)` buttons with `delay={1000}`, when `open()` is invoked, run 60% through the warmup delay, and then invoked again, then the earlier warmup timer is cleared and replaced: the tooltip does not appear at 40% into the second call (which would be enough time left over from the first call's timer) and only opens once the full 1000ms has elapsed from the second call. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "clears previous warmup timeout when open is called multiple times rapidly")
- Given the bare hooks with a trigger whose `onMouseEnter` handler calls `state.open(false)` in addition to the hook's own `triggerProps.onMouseEnter`, when the trigger is hovered with `delay={1000}`, then the tooltip still is not visible halfway through the delay and only opens once the full delay elapses, i.e. the redundant `open()` call during warmup does not shorten or skip the delay. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/tooltip/useTooltipTriggerState.test.js: "does not open immediately when open() is called twice during warmup")

## toast

### Roles and attributes

- Given a React Aria Components `ToastRegion`/`Toast` triggered so one toast is visible, when the region is queried, then it has role="region" with `aria-label="1 notification."` and class `react-aria-ToastRegion`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should trigger a toast")
- Given that same visible toast, when the toast element is queried, then it has role="alertdialog", class `react-aria-Toast`, `aria-modal="false"`, an `aria-labelledby` pointing at the title text ("Toast") and an `aria-describedby` pointing at the description text ("Description"). (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should trigger a toast")
- Given that same visible toast, when its content is inspected, then it contains a nested role="alert" element with class `react-aria-ToastContent`, and a close button with `aria-label="Close"`; when that close button is clicked, then both the alertdialog and alert roles disappear from the document. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should trigger a toast")
- Given a React Aria Components `ToastRegion` given an explicit `aria-label="Toasts"`, when a toast is added, then the region's `aria-label` is "Toasts" instead of the default localized count string. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should support custom aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` rendered with a button that triggers `ToastQueue.neutral`, when the button is clicked, then the region has `aria-label="1 notification."`, the alertdialog and its nested alert become visible, and the toast's close button has `aria-label="Close"`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "renders a button that triggers a toast")
- Given that same Spectrum v3 toast, when its close button is clicked, then both the alertdialog and alert roles are removed from the document. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "renders a button that triggers a toast")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` with `data-testid` passed through `RenderToastButton` props, when a toast with `actionLabel="Update"` is triggered, then the container element is found by that `data-testid`, and the rendered toast's secondary button and close button are found by the fixed test ids `rsp-Toast-secondaryButton` and `rsp-Toast-closeButton`. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "supports testid prop")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` triggering a `variant="positive"` toast, when the toast is shown, then its icon has role="img" with `aria-label="Success"`, and the toast's `aria-labelledby` points at the rendered title text's id. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should label icon by variant")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` given an explicit `aria-label="Toasts"`, when a toast is added, then the region's `aria-label` is "Toasts" instead of the default localized count string. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should support custom aria-label")
- Given a Spectrum 2 (`@react-spectrum/s2`) `ToastContainer` triggered via `ToastQueue.neutral` with `id="toast-1"` and `data-testid="toast-1"` passed through the toast options, when the toast is shown, then the rendered alertdialog element itself carries `id="toast-1"` and `data-testid="toast-1"`, and is the same element found by that test id. (react-spectrum: packages/@react-spectrum/s2/test/Toast.test.tsx: "passes an id and data attribute through to the rendered toast")
- Given `useToast({toast: {}}, state, ref)`, when the returned props are inspected, then `toastProps.role` is `alertdialog`, `contentProps.role` is `alert`, `closeButtonProps['aria-label']` is the localized `Close`, `closeButtonProps.onPress` is a function, and `titleProps.id` equals `toastProps['aria-labelledby']`. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/toast/useToast.test.js: "handles defaults")

### Keyboard

- Given a React Aria Components `ToastRegion` with a visible toast and focus still on the trigger button, when the F6 key is pressed, then focus moves to the toast region element. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "can focus toast region using F6")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` with a visible toast and focus still on the trigger button, when the F6 key is fired (keydown/keyup), then focus moves to the toast region element. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "can focus toast region using F6")
- Given the `useToast` storybook example configured to show a single toast at a time, when two toasts are queued with Enter, then only the newest ("Mmmmm toast 2x") is rendered as the `alertdialog`; pressing F6 moves into the toast region, tabbing reaches its close button, and Enter dismisses it; the next queued toast ("Mmmmm toast 1x") then renders and receives focus itself; dismissing that one too returns focus to the original trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria/test/toast/useToast.test.js: "moves focus to the next toast when it appears")

### Pointer (mouse, touch, pen, virtual click from screen readers)

- Given a React Aria Components toast with `timeout={5000}`, when the toast is hovered partway through its timeout, then the dismissal timer pauses so the toast is still visible even 7 seconds later; when the pointer unhovers, then the remaining timeout resumes and the toast is removed after the leftover time elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "pauses timers when hovering")
- Given a React Aria Components `ToastRegion` where the trigger button is clicked twice to queue two toasts, when the close button inside the first visible toast is clicked, then focus moves to the trigger button and the next toast becomes visible; when its close button is also clicked, then no alertdialog remains and focus stays on the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should restore focus when removing with the mouse")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast with `timeout={5000}`, when the toast is hovered partway through its timeout, then the dismissal timer pauses so the toast is still visible 7 seconds later; when the pointer unhovers, then the remaining timeout resumes and the toast is removed after the leftover time elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "pauses timers when hovering")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` where the trigger button is clicked twice to queue two toasts, when the close button inside the first visible toast is clicked, then focus moves to the trigger button and the next toast becomes visible; when its close button is also clicked, then no alertdialog remains and focus stays on the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should restore focus when removing with the mouse")

### Focus management

- Given a React Aria Components toast with `timeout={5000}`, when the close button inside the toast receives focus partway through the timeout, then the dismissal timer pauses so the toast is still visible 7 seconds later; when the button is blurred, then the remaining timeout resumes and the toast is removed after the leftover time elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "pauses timers when focusing")
- Given a React Aria Components toast opened by clicking a trigger button, when the toast's close button is clicked so the toast exits, then focus is restored to the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should restore focus when a toast exits")
- Given a React Aria Components `ToastRegion` with two toasts queued via keyboard (Enter pressed twice on the trigger), when F6 then Tab-Tab moves focus to the first toast's close button and Enter is pressed to close it, then focus moves onto the remaining toast itself (`document.activeElement` is the toast); when Tab then Enter closes that toast's close button too, then no alertdialog remains and focus returns to the original trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should move focus to remaining toast when a toast exits and there are more")
- Given the same two-toasts-via-keyboard setup, when the first toast is closed and focus lands on the remaining toast, then that remaining toast carries `data-focused="true"`; when it is subsequently closed too, then focus returns to the trigger button and no toast remains. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should move focus from the last toast to remaining toast when a the last toast is closed")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast with `timeout={5000}`, when the toast's close button receives focus partway through the timeout, then the dismissal timer pauses so the toast is still visible 7 seconds later; when the button is blurred, then the remaining timeout resumes and the toast is removed after the leftover time elapses. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "pauses timers when focusing")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast opened by clicking a trigger button, when the toast's close button is clicked so the toast exits, then focus is restored to the trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should restore focus when a toast exits")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` with two toasts queued via keyboard (tab to trigger, Enter pressed twice), when F6 then Tab-Tab moves focus to the first toast's close button and Enter is pressed to close it, then focus moves onto the remaining toast itself; when Tab then Enter closes that toast's close button too, then no alertdialog remains and focus returns to the original trigger button. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should move focus to remaining toast when a toast exits and there are more")
- Given the same Spectrum v3 two-toasts-via-keyboard setup, when the first toast is closed and focus lands on the remaining toast, then closing that remaining toast too returns focus to the trigger button with no toast left. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should move focus from the last toast to remaining toast when a the last toast is closed")

### State and controlled/uncontrolled

- Given a React Aria Components toast created with `timeout={5000}`, when enough fake-timer time elapses past the timeout, then the toast (role="alertdialog") is removed from the document without any user action. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "removes a toast via timeout")
- Given a React Aria Components setup where a button toggles a toast via `ToastQueue.add`/`queue.close(key)` tracked in local state, when the button is pressed to add a toast, then the alertdialog and its nested alert are visible; when the button is pressed again to call `queue.close(key)`, then both the alertdialog and alert roles are removed. (react-spectrum: packages/react-aria-components/test/Toast.test.js: "should support programmatically closing toasts")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast created with `timeout={5000}`, when enough fake-timer time elapses past the timeout, then the toast is removed from the document without any user action. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "removes a toast via timeout")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast rendered with `actionLabel`, `onAction`, and `onClose` but no `shouldCloseOnAction`, when the action button is clicked, then `onAction` fires once and `onClose` is never called (the toast is not dismissed by the action). (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "renders a toast with an action")
- Given a Spectrum v3 `ToastContainer` toast rendered with `actionLabel`, `onAction`, `onClose`, and `shouldCloseOnAction`, when the action button is clicked, then `onAction` and `onClose` each fire once and the alertdialog is removed from the document. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "closes toast on action")
- Given a Spectrum v3 setup where a button toggles a toast by calling `ToastQueue.positive(...)` (which returns a close function) tracked in local state, when the button is pressed to show the toast then pressed again to invoke the returned close function, then the alertdialog and alert are removed. (react-spectrum: packages/@adobe/react-spectrum/test/toast/ToastContainer.test.js: "should support programmatically closing toasts")
- Given the bare `useToastState` hook with no options, when `add` is called with a message and `{timeout: 0}`, then `visibleToasts` grows from empty to one entry whose `content` matches, whose `timeout` is 0, whose `timer` is undefined, and which has a `key` property. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/toast/useToastState.test.js: "should add a new toast via add")
- Given the bare `useToastState` hook, when `add` is called with `{timeout: 5000}`, then the resulting toast entry has a defined `timer` (unlike a toast added with `timeout: 0`). (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/toast/useToastState.test.js: "should add a new toast with a timer")
- Given the bare `useToastState` hook configured with `maxVisibleToasts: 2`, when a second toast is added after a first, then `visibleToasts` has length 2 with the newest toast first and the older one second. (react-spectrum: packages/react-stately/test/toast/useToastState.test.js: "should be able to add multiple toasts")
